Again, the responsibilities of tactical coordination and navigation officers serving in naval outpost aircraft are extended in terms of military service. It is possible that an additional payment will be paid per month to those who have not received additional monthly medical and dental pensions under Article 5510, which, along with their monthly compensation, will be multiplied by the official monthly coefficient of 17,000 indicators for those who are not qualified to receive additional medical and dental pensions, as well as by the employee's pensions per month. In addition, in exchange for special medical services such as organ transplants, cancer surgery, additional payment in return capital is allowed to increase the ceiling rate, and in a similar way to increase the return capital share paid to healthcare workers in the field of health tourism. Medical professors are also allowed to receive additional funding under specific medical procedures and given international health care, and to implement a 60-day operational service increase in their year-long work for healthcare professionals. Dear lawmakers, the law proposal prevents revenue from illegal betting from being used as financial support for terrorist organisations, there are regulations for this. Again, the bonus in the games of chance is envisioned to increase the gross upper limit from 59 per cent to 83 per cent. In the field of international health services, an anonymous company is established to promote services offered in our country, support and co-ordinate the activities of the public and private sector for health tourism, offer policy and services standards for international healthcare services, and offer recommendations to the Ministry of Health for Akreditation criteria, such as the International Health Services Corporation.
